   163→        shell: powershell
   164→        run: |
   165→          $url = "https://github.com/winfsp/winfsp/releases/download/v2.1/winfsp-2.1.25156.msi"
   166→          $out = "winfsp.msi"
   167→          $expectedHash = "073A70E00F77423E34BED98B86E600DEF93393BA5822204FAC57A29324DB9F7A"
   168→          Invoke-WebRequest -Uri $url -OutFile $out
   169→          $actualHash = (Get-FileHash $out -Algorithm SHA256).Hash
   170→          if ($actualHash -ne $expectedHash) { throw "WinFsp MSI hash mismatch: expected $expectedHash, got $actualHash" }
   171→          Start-Process msiexec.exe -ArgumentList "/i","$out","/qn","INSTALLLEVEL=1000" -Wait -NoNewWindow
   172→
   173→      # --- Setup Node.js + pnpm ---
   174→
   175→      - uses: pnpm/action-setup@v4
   176→        with:
   177→          version: 10
   178→
   179→      - uses: actions/setup-node@v4
   180→        with:
   181→          node-version: '22'
   182→          cache: 'pnpm'
   183→
   184→      - name: Install dependencies
   185→        run: pnpm install --frozen-lockfile
   186→
   187→      - name: Build mock-ipns-routing
   188→        run: cd tools/mock-ipns-routing && npm install && npm run build
   189→
   190→      - name: Build packages
   191→        run: pnpm --filter @cipherbox/crypto --filter @cipherbox/api build
   192→
   193→      # --- Configuration ---
   194→
   195→      - name: Create API .env
   196→        shell: bash
   197→        run: |
   198→          cat > apps/api/.env << 'ENVEOF'
   199→          NODE_ENV=test
   200→          DB_HOST=localhost
   201→          DB_PORT=5432
   202→          DB_USERNAME=postgres
   203→          DB_PASSWORD=postgres
   204→          DB_DATABASE=cipherbox_test
   205→          JWT_SECRET=[REDACTED]
   206→          CORS_ALLOWED_ORIGINS=http://localhost:5173
   207→          IPFS_PROVIDER=local
   208→          IPFS_LOCAL_API_URL=http://localhost:5001
   209→          IPFS_LOCAL_GATEWAY_URL=http://localhost:8080
   210→          DELEGATED_ROUTING_URL=http://localhost:3001
   211→          REDIS_HOST=localhost
   212→          REDIS_PORT=6379
   213→          TEST_LOGIN_SECRET=[REDACTED]
   214→          ENVEOF
   215→          echo "IDENTITY_JWT_PRIVATE_KEY=${{ secrets.IDENTITY_JWT_PRIVATE_KEY }}" >> apps/api/.env
   216→
   217→      - name: Create desktop .env
   218→        shell: bash
   219→        run: |
   220→          cat > apps/desktop/.env << 'ENVEOF'
   221→          VITE_API_URL=http://localhost:3000
   222→          VITE_TEST_LOGIN_SECRET=[REDACTED]
   223→          ENVEOF
   224→
   225→      # --- Database + API startup ---
   226→
   227→      - name: Run migrations
   228→        run: pnpm --filter @cipherbox/api migration:run
   229→        env:
   230→          DB_HOST: localhost
   231→          DB_PORT: 5432
   232→          DB_USERNAME: postgres
   233→          DB_PASSWORD: postgres
   234→          DB_DATABASE: cipherbox_test
   235→          NODE_ENV: test
   236→
   237→      - name: Start mock IPNS routing
   238→        shell: bash
   239→        run: node tools/mock-ipns-routing/dist/index.js &
   240→
   241→      - name: Start API server
   242→        if: runner.os != 'Windows'
   243→        run: |
   244→          cd apps/api
   245→          node dist/main.js &
   246→          # Wait for API readiness
   247→          for i in $(seq 1 30); do
   248→            if curl -s http://localhost:3000/health > /dev/null 2>&1; then
   249→              echo "API ready"
   250→              break
   251→            fi
   252→            sleep 1
   253→          done
   254→        env:
   255→          NODE_ENV: test
   256→          DB_HOST: localhost
   257→          DB_PORT: 5432
   258→          DB_USERNAME: postgres
   259→          DB_PASSWORD: postgres
   260→          DB_DATABASE: cipherbox_test
   261→          JWT_SECRET=[REDACTED]
   262→          CORS_ALLOWED_ORIGINS: http://localhost:5173
   263→          IPFS_PROVIDER: local
   264→          IPFS_LOCAL_API_URL: http://localhost:5001
   265→          IPFS_LOCAL_GATEWAY_URL: http://localhost:8080
   266→          DELEGATED_ROUTING_URL: http://localhost:3001
   267→          REDIS_HOST: localhost
   268→          REDIS_PORT: 6379
   269→          TEST_LOGIN_SECRET=[REDACTED]
   270→          IDENTITY_JWT_PRIVATE_KEY: ${{ secrets.IDENTITY_JWT_PRIVATE_KEY }}
   271→
   272→      - name: Start API server (Windows)
   273→        if: runner.os == 'Windows'
   274→        shell: powershell
   275→        run: |
   276→          $env:NODE_ENV = "test"
   277→          $env:DB_HOST = "localhost"
   278→          $env:DB_PORT = "5432"
   279→          $env:DB_USERNAME = "postgres"
   280→          $env:DB_PASSWORD = "postgres"
   281→          $env:DB_DATABASE = "cipherbox_test"
   282→          $env:JWT_SECRET=[REDACTED]"
   283→          $env:CORS_ALLOWED_ORIGINS = "http://localhost:5173"
   284→          $env:IPFS_PROVIDER = "local"
   285→          $env:IPFS_LOCAL_API_URL = "http://localhost:5001"
   286→          $env:IPFS_LOCAL_GATEWAY_URL = "http://localhost:8080"
   287→          $env:DELEGATED_ROUTING_URL = "http://localhost:3001"
   288→          $env:REDIS_HOST = "localhost"
   289→          $env:REDIS_PORT = "6379"
   290→          $env:TEST_LOGIN_SECRET=[REDACTED]"
   291→          $env:IDENTITY_JWT_PRIVATE_KEY = "${{ secrets.IDENTITY_JWT_PRIVATE_KEY }}"
   292→          Start-Process -FilePath node -ArgumentList "dist/main.js" -WorkingDirectory "apps/api" -NoNewWindow
   293→          # Wait for API readiness
   294→          for ($i = 0; $i -lt 30; $i++) {
   295→            try {
   296→              $null = Invoke-WebRequest -Uri "http://localhost:3000/health" -ErrorAction Stop
   297→              Write-Host "API ready"
   298→              break
   299→            } catch { Start-Sleep -Seconds 1 }
   300→          }
   301→
   302→      # --- Desktop launch + test execution ---
   303→
   304→      - name: Run desktop E2E tests (macOS)
   305→        if: runner.os == 'macOS'
   306→        run: |
   307→          export CIPHERBOX_API_URL=http://localhost:3000
   308→          export VITE_API_URL=http://localhost:3000
   309→          export VITE_TEST_LOGIN_SECRET=[REDACTED]
   310→          export RUST_LOG=info
   311→          DEV_KEY=$(openssl rand -hex 32)
   312→          ./desktop-binary/cipherbox-desktop --dev-key $DEV_KEY &
   313→          DESKTOP_PID=$!
   314→          bash tests/e2e-desktop/scripts/run-all.sh
   315→          TEST_EXIT=$?
   316→          kill $DESKTOP_PID 2>/dev/null || true
   317→          diskutil unmount force $HOME/CipherBox 2>/dev/null || true
   318→          exit $TEST_EXIT
   319→
   320→      - name: Run desktop E2E tests (Linux)
   321→        if: runner.os == 'Linux'
   322→        uses: GabrielBB/xvfb-action@v1
   323→        with:
   324→          run: |
   325→            export CIPHERBOX_API_URL=http://localhost:3000
   326→            export VITE_API_URL=http://localhost:3000
   327→            export VITE_TEST_LOGIN_SECRET=[REDACTED]
   328→            export RUST_LOG=info
   329→            DEV_KEY=$(openssl rand -hex 32)
   330→            ./desktop-binary/cipherbox-desktop --dev-key $DEV_KEY &
   331→            DESKTOP_PID=$!
   332→            bash tests/e2e-desktop/scripts/run-all.sh
   333→            TEST_EXIT=$?
   334→            kill $DESKTOP_PID 2>/dev/null || true
   335→            fusermount3 -u $HOME/CipherBox 2>/dev/null || true
   336→            exit $TEST_EXIT
   337→
   338→      - name: Run desktop E2E tests (Windows)
   339→        if: runner.os == 'Windows'
   340→        shell: powershell
   341→        run: |
   342→          $env:CIPHERBOX_API_URL = "http://localhost:3000"
   343→          $env:VITE_API_URL = "http://localhost:3000"
   344→          $env:VITE_TEST_LOGIN_SECRET=[REDACTED]"
   345→          $env:RUST_LOG = "info"
   346→          $DEV_KEY = -join ((1..32) | ForEach-Object { '{0:x2}' -f (Get-Random -Max 256) })
   347→          $proc = Start-Process -FilePath ".\desktop-binary\cipherbox-desktop.exe" -ArgumentList "--dev-key",$DEV_KEY -PassThru -NoNewWindow
   348→          & "$PWD\tests\e2e-desktop\scripts\run-all.ps1"
   349→          $testExit = $LASTEXITCODE
   350→          Stop-Process -Id $proc.Id -Force -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
   351→          exit $testExit
   352→
   353→      # --- Failure artifacts ---
   354→
   355→      - name: Upload logs on failure
   356→        if: failure()
   357→        uses: actions/upload-artifact@v4
   358→        with:
   359→          name: e2e-desktop-logs-${{ matrix.platform }}
   360→          path: |
   361→            apps/api/*.log
   362→            ~/Library/Logs/fuse-t/fuse-t.log
   363→          retention-days: 7
   364→
```

     1→# run-all.ps1 -- Orchestrate the full desktop E2E test suite (Windows).
     2→#
     3→# Runs wait-for-mount, FUSE file I/O tests, and API round-trip tests.
     4→# Reports an aggregate pass/fail summary and exits with the total failure count.
     5→#
     6→# Environment:
     7→#   MOUNT_POINT   Path to FUSE mount (default: $env:USERPROFILE\CipherBox)
     8→#   API_URL       Backend API URL (default: http://localhost:3000)
     9→#   TEST_SECRET   test-login shared secret (default: e2e-test-secret-ci-only)
    10→
    11→$ErrorActionPreference = "Stop"
    12→
    13→$MountPoint = if ($env:MOUNT_POINT) { $env:MOUNT_POINT } else { "$env:USERPROFILE\CipherBox" }
    14→$ApiUrl = if ($env:API_URL) { $env:API_URL } else { "http://localhost:3000" }
    15→$TestSecret = if ($env:TEST_SECRET) { $env:TEST_SECRET } else { "e2e-test-secret-ci-only" }
    16→
    17→$TotalFail = 0
    18→
    19→Write-Host "============================================"
    20→Write-Host "  CipherBox Desktop E2E Test Suite"
    21→Write-Host "============================================"
    22→Write-Host ""
    23→Write-Host "Mount point: $MountPoint"
    24→Write-Host "API URL:     $ApiUrl"
    25→Write-Host ""
    26→
    27→# ---- Step 1: Wait for mount ----
    28→Write-Host "--- Step 1: Wait for mount ---"
    29→try {
    30→    & "$PSScriptRoot\wait-for-mount.ps1" -MountPoint $MountPoint
    31→} catch {
    32→    Write-Host "FATAL: Mount not available. Cannot continue."
    33→    exit 1
    34→}
    35→Write-Host ""
    36→
    37→# ---- Step 2: FUSE file operations ----
    38→Write-Host "--- Step 2: FUSE file operations ---"
    39→$FuseExitCode = 0
    40→try {
    41→    & "$PSScriptRoot\test-fuse-operations.ps1" -MountPoint $MountPoint
    42→    $FuseExitCode = $LASTEXITCODE
    43→} catch {
    44→    $FuseExitCode = 1
    45→}
    46→
    47→if ($FuseExitCode -eq 0) {
    48→    Write-Host "FUSE operations: ALL PASSED"
    49→} else {
    50→    Write-Host "FUSE operations: $FuseExitCode FAILURE(S)"
    51→    $TotalFail += $FuseExitCode
    52→}
    53→Write-Host ""
    54→
    55→# ---- Step 3: API round-trip ----
    56→Write-Host "--- Step 3: API round-trip ---"
    57→$RtExitCode = 0
    58→try {
    59→    & "$PSScriptRoot\test-round-trip.ps1" -MountPoint $MountPoint -ApiUrl $ApiUrl -TestSecret $TestSecret
    60→    $RtExitCode = $LASTEXITCODE
    61→} catch {
    62→    $RtExitCode = 1
    63→}
    64→
    65→if ($RtExitCode -eq 0) {
    66→    Write-Host "API round-trip: ALL PASSED"
    67→} else {
    68→    Write-Host "API round-trip: $RtExitCode FAILURE(S)"
    69→    $TotalFail += $RtExitCode
    70→}
    71→Write-Host ""
    72→
    73→# ---- Summary ----
    74→Write-Host "============================================"
    75→Write-Host "  Summary"
    76→Write-Host "============================================"
    77→Write-Host "  Total failures: $TotalFail"
    78→Write-Host "============================================"
    79→
    80→exit $TotalFail
    81→

     1→#!/usr/bin/env bash
     2→set -euo pipefail
     3→
     4→# run-all.sh -- Orchestrate the full desktop E2E test suite.
     5→#
     6→# Runs wait-for-mount, FUSE file I/O tests, and API round-trip tests.
     7→# Reports an aggregate pass/fail summary and exits with the total failure count.
     8→#
     9→# Environment:
    10→#   MOUNT_POINT   Path to FUSE mount (default: $HOME/CipherBox)
    11→#   API_URL       Backend API URL (default: http://localhost:3000)
    12→#   TEST_SECRET   test-login shared secret (default: e2e-test-secret-ci-only)
    13→
    14→MOUNT_POINT="${MOUNT_POINT:-$HOME/CipherBox}"
    15→API_URL="${API_URL:-http://localhost:3000}"
    16→TEST_SECRET="${TEST_SECRET=[REDACTED]}"
    17→
    18→SCRIPT_DIR="$(cd "$(dirname "$0")" && pwd)"
    19→
    20→TOTAL_PASS=0
    21→TOTAL_FAIL=0
    22→
    23→echo "============================================"
    24→echo "  CipherBox Desktop E2E Test Suite"
    25→echo "============================================"
    26→echo ""
    27→echo "Mount point: $MOUNT_POINT"
    28→echo "API URL:     $API_URL"
    29→echo ""
    30→
    31→# ---- Step 1: Wait for mount ----
    32→echo "--- Step 1: Wait for mount ---"
    33→if bash "$SCRIPT_DIR/wait-for-mount.sh" "$MOUNT_POINT"; then
    34→  TOTAL_PASS=$((TOTAL_PASS + 1))
    35→else
    36→  echo "FATAL: Mount not available. Cannot continue."
    37→  exit 1
    38→fi
    39→echo ""
    40→
    41→# ---- Step 2: FUSE file operations ----
    42→echo "--- Step 2: FUSE file operations ---"
    43→set +e
    44→bash "$SCRIPT_DIR/test-fuse-operations.sh" "$MOUNT_POINT"
    45→FUSE_FAILURES=$?
    46→set -e
    47→
    48→if [ "$FUSE_FAILURES" -eq 0 ]; then
    49→  echo "FUSE operations: ALL PASSED"
    50→else
    51→  echo "FUSE operations: $FUSE_FAILURES FAILURE(S)"
    52→  TOTAL_FAIL=$((TOTAL_FAIL + FUSE_FAILURES))
    53→fi
    54→echo ""
    55→
    56→# ---- Step 3: API round-trip ----
    57→echo "--- Step 3: API round-trip ---"
    58→set +e
    59→bash "$SCRIPT_DIR/test-round-trip.sh" "$MOUNT_POINT" "$API_URL" "$TEST_SECRET"
    60→RT_FAILURES=$?
    61→set -e
    62→
    63→if [ "$RT_FAILURES" -eq 0 ]; then
    64→  echo "API round-trip: ALL PASSED"
    65→else
    66→  echo "API round-trip: $RT_FAILURES FAILURE(S)"
    67→  TOTAL_FAIL=$((TOTAL_FAIL + RT_FAILURES))
    68→fi
    69→echo ""
    70→
    71→# ---- Summary ----
    72→echo "============================================"
    73→echo "  Summary"
    74→echo "============================================"
    75→echo "  Total failures: $TOTAL_FAIL"
    76→echo "============================================"
    77→
    78→exit "$TOTAL_FAIL"
    79→
```

     1→# test-fuse-operations.ps1 -- Exercise FUSE mount file I/O operations (Windows).
     2→#
     3→# Usage: .\test-fuse-operations.ps1 [-MountPoint <path>]
     4→#   -MountPoint  Path to FUSE mount (default: $env:USERPROFILE\CipherBox)
     5→#
     6→# Tests: create, read, overwrite, mkdir, nested write, binary round-trip,
     7→#        rename, delete file, delete directory.
     8→#
     9→# Exit code: number of failed tests (0 = all passed).
    10→
    11→param(
    12→    [string]$MountPoint = "$env:USERPROFILE\CipherBox"
    13→)
    14→
    15→$ErrorActionPreference = "Continue"
    16→
    17→$Pass = 0
    18→$Fail = 0
    19→
    20→function Test-Pass {
    21→    param([string]$Name)
    22→    $script:Pass++
    23→    Write-Host "PASS: $Name"
    24→}
    25→
    26→function Test-Fail {
    27→    param([string]$Name)
    28→    $script:Fail++
    29→    Write-Host "FAIL: $Name"
    30→}
    31→
    32→Write-Host "=== FUSE File I/O Tests ==="
    33→Write-Host "Mount point: $MountPoint"
    34→Write-Host ""
    35→
    36→# ---- Test 1: Create and read text file ----
    37→Write-Host "--- Test 1: Create and read text file ---"
    38→Set-Content -Path "$MountPoint\e2e-test.txt" -Value "Hello from CI" -NoNewline
    39→Start-Sleep -Seconds 3
    40→$Content = Get-Content -Path "$MountPoint\e2e-test.txt" -Raw
    41→if ($Content.Trim() -eq "Hello from CI") {
    42→    Test-Pass "Create and read text file"
    43→} else {
    44→    Test-Fail "Create and read text file (got: '$Content')"
    45→}
    46→
    47→# ---- Test 2: Create directory ----
    48→Write-Host "--- Test 2: Create directory ---"
    49→New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path "$MountPoint\e2e-folder" -Force | Out-Null
    50→if (Test-Path "$MountPoint\e2e-folder" -PathType Container) {
    51→    Test-Pass "Create directory"
    52→} else {
    53→    Test-Fail "Create directory"
    54→}
    55→
    56→# ---- Test 3: Write file in subdirectory ----
    57→Write-Host "--- Test 3: Write file in subdirectory ---"
    58→Set-Content -Path "$MountPoint\e2e-folder\nested.txt" -Value "Nested content" -NoNewline
    59→Start-Sleep -Seconds 3
    60→$Nested = Get-Content -Path "$MountPoint\e2e-folder\nested.txt" -Raw
    61→if ($Nested.Trim() -eq "Nested content") {
    62→    Test-Pass "Write file in subdirectory"
    63→} else {
    64→    Test-Fail "Write file in subdirectory (got: '$Nested')"
    65→}
    66→
    67→# ---- Test 4: Overwrite file (modify) ----
    68→Write-Host "--- Test 4: Overwrite file ---"
    69→Set-Content -Path "$MountPoint\e2e-test.txt" -Value "Modified content" -NoNewline
    70→Start-Sleep -Seconds 3
    71→$Modified = Get-Content -Path "$MountPoint\e2e-test.txt" -Raw
    72→if ($Modified.Trim() -eq "Modified content") {
    73→    Test-Pass "Overwrite file"
    74→} else {
    75→    Test-Fail "Overwrite file (got: '$Modified')"
    76→}
    77→
    78→# ---- Test 5: Binary file round-trip ----
    79→Write-Host "--- Test 5: Binary file round-trip ---"
    80→$TempBinary = "$env:TEMP\e2e-binary.bin"
    81→$MountBinary = "$MountPoint\e2e-binary.bin"
    82→$Bytes = New-Object byte[] (256 * 1024)
    83→[System.Security.Cryptography.RandomNumberGenerator]::Fill($Bytes)
    84→[System.IO.File]::WriteAllBytes($TempBinary, $Bytes)
    85→Copy-Item -Path $TempBinary -Destination $MountBinary
    86→Start-Sleep -Seconds 5
    87→$HashOrig = (Get-FileHash -Path $TempBinary -Algorithm SHA256).Hash
    88→$HashMount = (Get-FileHash -Path $MountBinary -Algorithm SHA256).Hash
    89→if ($HashOrig -eq $HashMount) {
    90→    Test-Pass "Binary file round-trip (256KB)"
    91→} else {
    92→    Test-Fail "Binary file round-trip (orig: $HashOrig, mount: $HashMount)"
    93→}
    94→
    95→# ---- Test 6: Rename file ----
    96→Write-Host "--- Test 6: Rename file ---"
    97→Move-Item -Path "$MountPoint\e2e-test.txt" -Destination "$MountPoint\e2e-renamed.txt" -Force
    98→Start-Sleep -Seconds 2
    99→$OldExists = Test-Path "$MountPoint\e2e-test.txt"
   100→$NewExists = Test-Path "$MountPoint\e2e-renamed.txt"
   101→if (-not $OldExists -and $NewExists) {
   102→    $Renamed = Get-Content -Path "$MountPoint\e2e-renamed.txt" -Raw
   103→    if ($Renamed.Trim() -eq "Modified content") {
   104→        Test-Pass "Rename file"
   105→    } else {
   106→        Test-Fail "Rename file (content changed: '$Renamed')"
   107→    }
   108→} else {
   109→    Test-Fail "Rename file (old exists: $OldExists, new exists: $NewExists)"
   110→}
   111→
   112→# ---- Test 7: Delete file ----
   113→Write-Host "--- Test 7: Delete file ---"
   114→Remove-Item -Path "$MountPoint\e2e-renamed.txt" -Force
   115→Start-Sleep -Seconds 2
   116→if (-not (Test-Path "$MountPoint\e2e-renamed.txt")) {
   117→    Test-Pass "Delete file"
   118→} else {
   119→    Test-Fail "Delete file (still exists)"
   120→}
   121→
   122→# ---- Test 8: Delete directory ----
   123→Write-Host "--- Test 8: Delete directory ---"
   124→Remove-Item -Path "$MountPoint\e2e-folder" -Recurse -Force
   125→Start-Sleep -Seconds 2
   126→if (-not (Test-Path "$MountPoint\e2e-folder")) {
   127→    Test-Pass "Delete directory"
   128→} else {
   129→    Test-Fail "Delete directory (still exists)"
   130→}
   131→
   132→# ---- Test 9: Cleanup ----
   133→Write-Host "--- Test 9: Cleanup ---"
   134→Remove-Item -Path $MountBinary -Force -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
   135→Remove-Item -Path $TempBinary -Force -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
   136→Test-Pass "Cleanup"
   137→
   138→# ---- Summary ----
   139→Write-Host ""
   140→Write-Host "=== FUSE File I/O Results ==="
   141→Write-Host "  Passed: $Pass"
   142→Write-Host "  Failed: $Fail"
   143→Write-Host "=============================="
   144→
   145→exit $Fail
   146→
```

     4→# test-fuse-operations.sh -- Exercise FUSE mount file I/O operations.

     2→#
     3→# Usage: .\test-round-trip.ps1 [-MountPoint <path>] [-ApiUrl <url>] [-TestSecret <secret>]
     4→#   -MountPoint   Path to FUSE mount (default: $env:USERPROFILE\CipherBox)
     5→#   -ApiUrl       Backend API URL (default: http://localhost:3000)
     6→#   -TestSecret   Shared secret for test-login (default: e2e-test-secret-ci-only)
     7→#
     8→# The server is zero-knowledge -- it cannot decrypt file contents.
     9→# These tests prove the pipeline: FUSE write -> encrypt -> IPFS upload -> IPNS publish -> API visibility.

    31→

     1→# wait-for-mount.ps1 -- Poll until the CipherBox FUSE mount point is available.
     2→#
     3→# Usage: .\wait-for-mount.ps1 [-MountPoint <path>]
     4→#   -MountPoint  Path to check (default: $env:USERPROFILE\CipherBox)
     5→#
     6→# Environment:
     7→#   MOUNT_TIMEOUT  Seconds to wait before failing (default: 90)
     8→
     9→param(
    10→    [string]$MountPoint = "$env:USERPROFILE\CipherBox"
    11→)
    12→
    13→$ErrorActionPreference = "Stop"
    14→
    15→$Timeout = if ($env:MOUNT_TIMEOUT) { [int]$env:MOUNT_TIMEOUT } else { 90 }
    16→$Interval = 2
    17→$Elapsed = 0
    18→
    19→Write-Host "Waiting for mount at $MountPoint (timeout: ${Timeout}s)..."
    20→
    21→while ($Elapsed -lt $Timeout) {
    22→    if (Test-Path $MountPoint) {
    23→        Write-Host "PASS: Mount detected at $MountPoint"
    24→        exit 0
    25→    }
    26→
    27→    Start-Sleep -Seconds $Interval
    28→    $Elapsed += $Interval
    29→}
    30→
    31→Write-Host "FAIL: Mount not detected after ${Timeout}s"
    32→exit 1
    33→

id: toolu_017CEesvwVtK8x736SQioRkY
```
     1→#!/usr/bin/env bash
     2→set -euo pipefail
     3→
     4→# wait-for-mount.sh -- Poll until the CipherBox FUSE mount point is available.
     5→#
     6→# Usage: ./wait-for-mount.sh [mount-point]
     7→#   mount-point  Path to check (default: $HOME/CipherBox)
     8→#
     9→# Environment:
    10→#   MOUNT_TIMEOUT  Seconds to wait before failing (default: 90)
    11→
    12→MOUNT_POINT="${1:-$HOME/CipherBox}"
    13→TIMEOUT="${MOUNT_TIMEOUT:-90}"
    14→INTERVAL=2
    15→ELAPSED=0
    16→
    17→echo "Waiting for mount at $MOUNT_POINT (timeout: ${TIMEOUT}s)..."
    18→
    19→while [ "$ELAPSED" -lt "$TIMEOUT" ]; do
    20→  # Check that the mount point directory exists AND that something is mounted there.
    21→  # macOS (FUSE-T SMB backend) shows "smbfs" in mount output; Linux shows "fuse".
    22→  # We grep for the mount path itself for cross-platform consistency.
    23→  if [ -d "$MOUNT_POINT" ] && mount | grep -q "$MOUNT_POINT"; then
    24→    echo "PASS: Mount detected at $MOUNT_POINT"
    25→    exit 0
    26→  fi
    27→
    28→  sleep "$INTERVAL"
    29→  ELAPSED=$((ELAPSED + INTERVAL))
    30→done
    31→
    32→echo "FAIL: Mount not detected after ${TIMEOUT}s"
    33→exit 1
    34→
```

   115→            -Headers $Headers
   116→
   117→        $ResolvedCid = $IpnsResponse.cid
   118→        if (-not $ResolvedCid) { $ResolvedCid = $IpnsResponse.value }
   119→
   120→        if ($ResolvedCid) {
   121→            Test-Pass "IPNS resolve returned CID ($ResolvedCid)"
   122→        } else {
   123→            Test-Fail "IPNS resolve did not return expected CID"
   124→        }
   125→    } catch {
   126→        Test-Fail "IPNS resolve failed ($_)"
   127→    }
   128→} else {
   129→    Test-Fail "IPNS resolve skipped (no rootIpnsName)"
```

> AGENT

Good, the flow is correct. If the FUSE write fails, `$RootIpns` is null, but the vault API call will still run and potentially set it. That's actually fine — the vault check is independent of the write check.

test-fuse-operations.ps1 -- Exercise FUSE mount file I/O operations (Windows).\n+#\n+# Usage: .\\test-fuse-operations.ps1 [-MountPoint <path>]\n+#   -MountPoint  Path to FUSE mount (default: $env:USERPROFILE\\CipherBox)\n+#\n+# Tests: create, read, overwrite, mkdir, nested write, binary round-trip,\n+#        rename, delete file, delete directory.\n+#\n+# Exit code: number of failed tests (0 = all passed).\n+\n+param(\n+    [string]$MountPoint = \"$env:USERPROFILE\\CipherBox\"\n+)\n+\n+$ErrorActionPreference = \"Continue\"\n+\n+$Pass = 0\n+$Fail = 0\n+\n+function Test-Pass {\n+    param([string]$Name)\n+    $script:Pass++\n+    Write-Host \"PASS: $Name\"\n+}\n+\n+function Test-Fail {\n+    param([string]$Name)\n+    $script:Fail++\n+    Write-Host \"FAIL: $Name\"\n+}\n+\n+Write-Host \"=== FUSE File I/O Tests ===\"\n+Write-Host \"Mount point: $MountPoint\"\n+Write-Host \"\"\n+\n+# ---- Test 1: Create and read text file ----\n+Write-Host \"--- Test 1: Create and read text file ---\"\n+Set-Content -Path \"$MountPoint\\e2e-test.txt\" -Value \"Hello from CI\" -NoNewline\n+Start-Sleep -Seconds 3\n+$Content = Get-Content -Path \"$MountPoint\\e2e-test.txt\" -Raw\n+if ($Content.Trim() -eq \"Hello from CI\") {\n+    Test-Pass \"Create and read text file\"\n+} else {\n+    Test-Fail \"Create and read text file (got: '$Content')\"\n+}\n+\n+# ---- Test 2: Create directory ----\n+Write-Host \"--- Test 2: Create directory ---\"\n+New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path \"$MountPoint\\e2e-folder\" -Force | Out-Null\n+if (Test-Path \"$MountPoint\\e2e-folder\" -PathType Container) {\n+    Test-Pass \"Create directory\"\n+} else {\n+    Test-Fail \"Create directory\"\n+}\n+\n+# ---- Test 3: Write file in subdirectory ----\n+Write-Host \"--- Test 3: Write file in subdirectory ---\"\n+Set-Content -Path \"$MountPoint\\e2e-folder\\nested.txt\" -Value \"Nested content\" -NoNewline\n+Start-Sleep -Seconds 3\n+$Nested = Get-Content -Path \"$MountPoint\\e2e-folder\\nested.txt\" -Raw\n+if ($Nested.Trim() -eq \"Nested content\") {\n+    Test-Pass \"Write file in subdirectory\"\n+} else {\n+    Test-Fail \"Write file in subdirectory (got: '$Nested')\"\n+}\n+\n+# ---- Test 4: Overwrite file (modify) ----\n+Write-Host \"--- Test 4: Overwrite file ---\"\n+Set-Content -Path \"$MountPoint\\e2e-test.txt\" -Value \"Modified content\" -NoNewline\n+Start-Sleep -Seconds 3\n+$Modified = Get-Content -Path \"$MountPoint\\e2e-test.txt\" -Raw\n+if ($Modified.Trim() -eq \"Modified content\") {\n+    Test-Pass \"Overwrite file\"\n+} else {\n+    Test-Fail \"Overwrite file (got: '$Modified')\"\n+}\n+\n+# ---- Test 5: Binary file round-trip ----\n+Write-Host \"--- Test 5: Binary file round-trip ---\"\n+$TempBinary = \"$env:TEMP\\e2e-binary.bin\"\n+$MountBinary = \"$MountPoint\\e2e-binary.bin\"\n+$Bytes = New-Object byte[] (256 * 1024)\n+[System.Security.Cryptography.RandomNumberGenerator]::Fill($Bytes)\n+[System.IO.File]::WriteAllBytes($TempBinary, $Bytes)\n+Copy-Item -Path $TempBinary -Destination $MountBinary\n+Start-Sleep -Seconds 5\n+$HashOrig = (Get-FileHash -Path $TempBinary -Algorithm SHA256).Hash\n+$HashMount = (Get-FileHash -Path $MountBinary -Algorithm SHA256).Hash\n+if ($HashOrig -eq $HashMount) {\n+    Test-Pass \"Binary file round-trip (256KB)\"\n+} else {\n+    Test-Fail \"Binary file round-trip (orig: $HashOrig, mount: $HashMount)\"\n+}\n+\n+# ---- Test 6: Rename file ----\n+Write-Host \"--- Test 6: Rename file ---\"\n+Move-Item -Path \"$MountPoint\\e2e-test.txt\" -Destination \"$MountPoint\\e2e-renamed.txt\" -Force\n+Start-Sleep -Seconds 2\n+$OldExists = Test-Path \"$MountPoint\\e2e-test.txt\"\n+$NewExists = Test-Path \"$MountPoint\\e2e-renamed.txt\"\n+if (-not $OldExists -and $NewExists) {\n+    $Renamed = Get-Content -Path \"$MountPoint\\e2e-renamed.txt\" -Raw\n+    if ($Renamed.Trim() -eq \"Modified content\") {\n+        Test-Pass \"Rename file\"\n+    } else {\n+        Test-Fail \"Rename file (content changed: '$Renamed')\"\n+    }\n+} else {\n+    Test-Fail \"Rename file (old exists: $OldExists, new exists: $NewExists)\"\n+}\n+\n+# ---- Test 7: Delete file ----\n+Write-Host \"--- Test 7: Delete file ---\"\n+Remove-Item -Path \"$MountPoint\\e2e-renamed.txt\" -Force\n+Start-Sleep -Seconds 2\n+if (-not (Test-Path \"$MountPoint\\e2e-renamed.txt\")) {\n+    Test-Pass \"Delete file\"\n+} else {\n+    Test-Fail \"Delete file (still exists)\"\n+}\n+\n+# ---- Test 8: Delete directory ----\n+Write-Host \"--- Test 8: Delete directory ---\"\n+Remove-Item -Path \"$MountPoint\\e2e-folder\" -Recurse -Force\n+Start-Sleep -Seconds 2\n+if (-not (Test-Path \"$MountPoint\\e2e-folder\")) {\n+    Test-Pass \"Delete directory\"\n+} else {\n+    Test-Fail \"Delete directory (still exists)\"\n+}\n+\n+# ---- Test 9: Cleanup ----\n+Write-Host \"--- Test 9: Cleanup ---\"\n+Remove-Item -Path $MountBinary -Force -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue\n+Remove-Item -Path $TempBinary -Force -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue\n+Test-Pass _⚠️ Potential issue_ | _🟡 Minor_

**FUSE write failure doesn't skip vault verification, potentially masking failures.**

When the FUSE write fails (lines 89-92), `$RootIpns` is set to `$null`, but the vault API call (lines 95-108) still executes and can overwrite `$RootIpns` with a value from a previous state. This could cause the test to pass even though the current write failed.


<details>
<summary>🔧 Proposed fix: track write success and gate subsequent tests</summary>

```diff
+$FuseWriteSucceeded = $false
 try {
     Set-Content -Path "$MountPoint\rt-test.txt" -Value "API-visible content" -NoNewline -ErrorAction Stop
     if (-not (Test-Path "$MountPoint\rt-test.txt")) { throw "FUSE write did not materialize at mount path" }
+    $FuseWriteSucceeded = $true
 } catch {
     Test-Fail "FUSE write failed ($_)"
-    $RootIpns = $null
 }
 Start-Sleep -Seconds 5

-try {
+if ($FuseWriteSucceeded) {
+  try {
     $VaultResponse = Invoke-RestMethod -Uri "$ApiUrl/vault" `
         -Headers $Headers

     $RootIpns = $VaultResponse.rootIpnsName
     if ($RootIpns) {
         Test-Pass "Vault has rootIpnsName after FUSE write ($RootIpns)"
     } else {
         Test-Fail "Vault has no rootIpnsName"
     }
-} catch {
+  } catch {
     Test-Fail "Vault API call failed ($_)"
     $RootIpns = $null
+  }
+} else {
+    Test-Fail "Vault verification skipped (FUSE write failed)"
+    $RootIpns = $null
 }
```
</details>

<details>
<summary>🤖 Prompt for AI Agents</summary>

```
Verify each finding against the current code and only fix it if needed.

In `@tests/e2e-desktop/scripts/test-round-trip.ps1` around lines 86 - 108, The
FUSE write failure path can still allow the vault API call to run and overwrite
$RootIpns, so add an explicit boolean flag (e.g., $WriteSucceeded) initialized
to $false; set it to $true only after the Set-Content/Test-Path block succeeds,
and only execute the Invoke-RestMethod/$VaultResponse block when $WriteSucceeded
is $true; ensure when the write fails you skip the vault verification (and keep
$RootIpns = $null) so the test cannot pass due to stale state.
```

</details>

visibility.\n+#\n+# Exit code: number of failed tests (0 = all passed).\n+\n+param(\n+    [string]$MountPoint = \"$env:USERPROFILE\\CipherBox\",\n+    [string]$ApiUrl = \"http://localhost:3000\"\n+)\n+\n+$TestSecret = if ($env:TEST_SECRET) { $env:TEST_SECRET } else { \"e2e-test-secret-ci-only\" }\n+\n+$ErrorActionPreference = \"Continue\"\n+\n+$TestEmail = \"e2e-desktop-rt-$([DateTimeOffset]::UtcNow.ToUnixTimeSeconds())@test.local\"\n+\n+$Pass = 0\n+$Fail = 0\n+\n+function Test-Pass {\n+    param([string]$Name)\n+    $script:Pass++\n+    Write-Host \"PASS: $Name\"\n+}\n+\n+function Test-Fail {\n+    param([string]$Name)\n+    $script:Fail++\n+    Write-Host \"FAIL: $Name\"\n+}\n+\n+Write-Host \"=== API Round-Trip Tests ===\"\n+Write-Host \"Mount point: $MountPoint\"\n+Write-Host \"API URL:     $ApiUrl\"\n+Write-Host \"Test email:  $TestEmail\"\n+Write-Host \"\"\n+\n+# ---- Test 1: Authenticate via test-login ----\n+Write-Host \"--- Test 1: Authenticate via test-login ---\"\n+$Body = @{\n+    email  = $TestEmail\n+    secret = $TestSecret\n+} | ConvertTo-Json\n+\n+try {\n+    $AuthResponse = Invoke-RestMethod -Uri \"$ApiUrl/auth/test-login\" `\n+        -Method Post `\n+        -ContentType \"application/json\" `\n+        -Body $Body\n+\n+    $AccessToken = $AuthResponse.accessToken\n+    if ($AccessToken) {\n+        Test-Pass \"Authenticate via test-login\"\n+    } else {\n+        Test-Fail \"Authenticate via test-login (no accessToken in response)\"\n+        Write-Host \"FATAL: Cannot proceed without authentication.\"\n+        Write-Host \"\"\n+        Write-Host \"=== API Round-Trip Results ===\"\n+        Write-Host \"  Passed: $Pass\"\n+        Write-Host \"  Failed: $Fail\"\n+        Write-Host \"===============================\"\n+        exit $Fail\n+    }\n+} catch {\n+    Test-Fail \"Authenticate via test-login ($_)\"\n+    Write-Host \"FATAL: Cannot proceed without authentication.\"\n+    Write-Host \"\"\n+    Write-Host \"=== API Round-Trip Results ===\"\n+    Write-Host \"  Passed: $Pass\"\n+    Write-Host \"  Failed: $Fail\"\n+    Write-Host \"===============================\"\n+    exit $Fail\n+}\n+\n+$Headers = @{ Authorization = \"Bearer $AccessToken\" }\n+\n+# ---- Test 2: Desktop writes file, API verifies vault exists ----\n+Write-Host \"--- Test 2: Verify vault has content after FUSE write ---\"\n+try {\n+    Set-Content -Path \"$MountPoint\\rt-test.txt\" -Value \"API-visible content\" -NoNewline -ErrorAction Stop\n+    if (-not (Test-Path \"$MountPoint\\rt-test.txt\")) { throw \"FUSE write did not materialize at mount path\" }\n+} catch {\n+    Test-Fail \"FUSE write failed ($_)\"\n+    $RootIpns = $null\n+}\n+Start-Sleep -Seconds 5\n+\n+try {\n+    $VaultResponse = Invoke-RestMethod -Uri \"$ApiUrl/vault\" `\n+        -Headers $Headers\n+\n+    $RootIpns = $VaultResponse.rootIpnsName\n+    if ($RootIpns) {\n+        Test-Pass \"Vault has rootIpnsName after FUSE write ($RootIpns)\"\n+    } else {\n+        Test-Fail \"Vault has no rootIpnsName\"\n+    }\n+} catch {\n+    Test-Fail \"Vault API call failed ($_)\"\n+    $RootIpns =      2→

     6→    types: [completed]
     7→    branches: [main]
     8→  workflow_dispatch:
     9→    inputs:
    10→      run_id:
    11→        description: 'CI workflow run ID to download artifacts from (optional, uses latest if empty)'
    12→        required: false
    13→        type: string
    14→
    15→permissions:
```

> AGENT

It's **not** part of the normal CI flow on feature branches. It runs in two scenarios:

1. **After CI succeeds on `main`** — triggered via `workflow_run` when the CI workflow completes successfully on the main branch (i.e., after a PR is merged)
2. **Manual dispatch** — via `workflow_dispatch` with an optional `run_id` parameter

So the flow is: feature branch CI builds the debug desktop binaries and uploads them as artifacts → PR merges to main → main CI runs → on success, `e2e-desktop.yml` triggers automatically, downloads those artifacts, and runs the desktop E2E tests across macOS/Windows/Linux.

It's intentionally decoupled from PR CI because it needs the compiled desktop binaries and full backend infrastructure (Postgres, IPFS, Redis, FUSE driver) which would be too heavy/slow for every PR push.

> AGENT

Found it. The error is:

```
The system library `fuse` required by crate `fuser` was not found.
The system library `osxfuse` required by crate `fuser` was not found.
```

The `tauri-action` step (which runs first) succeeds, but the separate "Build debug binary for E2E" step fails because it runs `cargo build` directly and the FUSE-T headers aren't found by `pkg-config`.

Let me check what's different between the two steps — the tauri-action step succeeds but the direct cargo build doesn't.

```
fn main() {
    // Register rustc cfg for switching between mount implementations.
    // When fuser MSRV is updated to v1.77 or above, we should switch from 'cargo:' to 'cargo::' syntax.
    println!(
        "cargo:rustc-check-cfg=cfg(fuser_mount_impl, values(\"pure-rust\", \"libfuse2\", \"libfuse3\"))"
    );

    #[cfg(all(not(feature = "libfuse"), not(target_os = "linux")))]
    unimplemented!("Building without libfuse is only supported on Linux");

    #[cfg(not(feature = "libfuse"))]
    {
        println!("cargo:rustc-cfg=fuser_mount_impl=\"pure-rust\"");
    }
    #[cfg(feature = "libfuse")]
    {
        if cfg!(target_os = "macos") {
            if pkg_config::Config::new()
                .atleast_version("2.6.0")
                .probe("fuse") // for macFUSE 4.x
                .map_err(|e| eprintln!("{e}"))
                .is_ok()
            {
                println!("cargo:rustc-cfg=fuser_mount_impl=\"libfuse2\"");
                println!("cargo:rustc-cfg=feature=\"macfuse-4-compat\"");
            } else {
                pkg_config::Config::new()
                    .atleast_version("2.6.0")
                    .probe("osxfuse") // for osxfuse 3.x
                    .map_err(|e| eprintln!("{e}"))
                    .unwrap();
                println!("cargo:rustc-cfg=fuser_mount_impl=\"libfuse2\"");
            }
        } else {
            // First try to link with libfuse3
            if pkg_config::Config::new()
                .atleast_version("3.0.0")
                .probe("fuse3")
                .map_err(|e| eprintln!("{e}"))
                .is_ok()
            {
                println!("cargo:rustc-cfg=fuser_mount_impl=\"libfuse3\"");
            } else {
                // Fallback to libfuse
                pkg_config::Config::new()
                    .atleast_version("2.6.0")
                    .probe("fuse")
                    .map_err(|e| eprintln!("{e}"))
                    .unwrap();
                println!("cargo:rustc-cfg=fuser_mount_impl=\"libfuse2\"");
            }
        }
    }
}
```

[features]
default = ["fuse"]
fuse = ["dep:fuser", "dep:unicode-normalization"]
winfsp = ["dep:winfsp", "dep:widestring"]

[dependencies]
tauri = { version = "2", features = ["tray-icon", "image-png", "image-ico"] }
tauri-plugin-deep-link = "2"
--
tauri-plugin-shell = "2"
tauri-plugin-notification = "2"
fuser = { version = "0.16", default-features = false, features = ["libfuse"], optional = true }
keyring = { version = "3", features = ["apple-native", "windows-native", "linux-native-sync-persistent"] }
reqwest = { version = "0.12", features = ["json", "rustls-tls", "multipart"] }
tokio = { version = "1", features = ["full"] }
serde = { version = "1", features = ["derive"] }
serde_json = "1"
--

[patch.crates-io]
# Patched fuser with loop-read for FUSE-T socket compatibility.
# FUSE-T communicates via Unix domain socket (not /dev/fuse), so large
# FUSE messages can arrive in fragments. Stock fuser does a single read()
# and fails with "Short read of FUSE request". This patch loop-reads
# until the full message is received.
fuser = { path = "vendor/fuser" }
```

> AGENT

The tauri-action doesn't show verbose build output. The key insight is that `tauri-action` runs `pnpm tauri build` — which uses Tauri's CLI. Tauri CLI likely runs `cargo build --release`, and there may be a cached artifact from the `Cargo Check (macOS)` step or the action may set env vars internally.

Actually, wait — let me re-examine. The tauri-action does a **release** build, and the "Build debug binary" does a **debug** build. The build script in fuser uses `pkg-config` crate at build time. Both should behave identically since pkg-config searches system paths. The question is why one finds `fuse.pc` and the other doesn't.

Let me check if there's a Rust cache action that might be restoring a cached build that already had the pkg-config result:

> AGENT

There it is. Line 547: `apps/desktop/src-tauri/target` is **cached**. Here's what's happening:

1. **Cache hit** from a previous successful run restores `target/` with the release build artifacts from `tauri-action`
2. **`tauri-action`** (release build) runs — fuser's build script has already been cached from a previous run where it found `fuse.pc`, so cargo skips running it again. The build succeeds.
3. **`cargo build` (debug build)** runs — this is a fresh debug profile build, so cargo needs to run fuser's build script again. This time `pkg-config` can't find `fuse.pc` because `PKG_CONFIG_PATH` isn't set and `/usr/local/lib/pkgconfig` isn't in the default search path on macOS GitHub runners.

The fix is to set `PKG_CONFIG_PATH` for the debug build step. Let me verify this theory by checking where FUSE-T puts `fuse.pc` on macOS runners:

> TOOL

tool_use Bash
id: toolu_01MSpxD8bhgXP7WPJf29drA8
```json
{
  "command": "pkg-config --variable pc_path pkg-config 2>/dev/null || echo \"no pc_path\" && echo \"---\" && ls /usr/local/lib/pkgconfig/fuse.pc 2>/dev/null && cat /usr/local/lib/pkgconfig/fuse.pc | head -5",
  "description": "Verify fuse.pc location"
}
```

> TOOL

tool_result
id: toolu_01MSpxD8bhgXP7WPJf29drA8
```
/opt/homebrew/lib/pkgconfig:/opt/homebrew/share/pkgconfig:/usr/local/lib/pkgconfig:/usr/lib/pkgconfig:/opt/homebrew/Library/Homebrew/os/mac/pkgconfig/26
---
/usr/local/lib/pkgconfig/fuse.pc
prefix=/usr/local
exec_prefix=${prefix}
libdir=${exec_prefix}/lib
includedir=${prefix}/include
```

> AGENT

Now I understand the issue. The **tauri-action** (release build) likely used cached artifacts from `target/` (restored by the cache step at line 542-549), so cargo skipped running fuser's build script. But the **debug build** step needs to run the build script fresh, and `pkg-config` can't find `fuse.pc` because `/usr/local/lib/pkgconfig` (where FUSE-T installs it) isn't in the default search path on macOS GitHub Actions ARM64 runners.

The fix is simple — set `PKG_CONFIG_PATH` on the debug build step:

> TOOL

tool_use Edit
id: toolu_014xaXY6xzzBb3jrMsYkYXqZ
```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/michael/Code/cipher-box/.github/workflows/ci.yml",
  "old_string": "      - name: Build debug binary for E2E\n        run: cargo build --manifest-path apps/desktop/src-tauri/Cargo.toml --no-default-features --features fuse\n\n      - name: Upload debug desktop binary\n        uses: actions/upload-artifact@v4\n        with:\n          name: desktop-debug-macos",
  "new_string": "      - name: Build debug binary for E2E\n        run: cargo build --manifest-path apps/desktop/src-tauri/Cargo.toml --no-default-features --features fuse\n        env:\n          PKG_CONFIG_PATH: /usr/local/lib/pkgconfig\n\n      - name: Upload debug desktop binary\n        uses: actions/upload-artifact@v4\n        with:\n          name: desktop-debug-macos"
}
```

> AGENT

**Root cause:** FUSE-T installs `fuse.pc` to `/usr/local/lib/pkgconfig/`, which isn't in the default `pkg-config` search path on macOS ARM64 GitHub Actions runners. The `tauri-action` release build succeeds because it reuses cached `target/` artifacts (skipping the fuser build script), but the debug build step runs the build script fresh and `pkg-config` can't find `fuse` or `osxfuse`.

**Fix:** Added `PKG_CONFIG_PATH: /usr/local/lib/pkgconfig` as an env var on the debug build step.

> DEVELOPER

Ok the macos build is failing on the debug build step. One thing I didn't realise is that having these debug build steps in the main ci workflow is really slowing things down.

Maybe it makes sense to implement something like this:
PR CI only runs the cargo checks (if changes are detected)
once merged to main, run the debug build, execute the e2e desktop suite of test, and only if these pass, then build the production releases. Happy to hear alternative proposals.

Perfect! I have everything I need to provide a comprehensive map of the CI/CD pipeline. Let me now provide the detailed analysis:

## Comprehensive CI/CD Workflow Map for CipherBox

### Overview
The repository has 8 GitHub workflows:
1. **ci.yml** — Main CI pipeline for code quality, builds, and desktop binaries
2. **e2e.yml** — Web app E2E tests
3. **e2e-desktop.yml** — Desktop E2E tests (triggered after CI succeeds)
4. **deploy-staging.yml** — Staging deployment (triggered by staging tags)
5. **tag-staging.yml** — Manual staging tag creation and deployment
6. **release-please.yml** — Automated release management
7. **pr-title.yml** — PR title validation
8. **claude-code.yml** — Claude Code agent integration

---

## 1. CI Pipeline (`ci.yml`)

**Trigger:** `push` to `main` or `pull_request` to `main`

### Job Dependency Graph:
```
changes (always)
  ├─→ lint (always, no deps)
  ├─→ typecheck (needs: changes) [conditional]
  ├─→ api-spec (needs: changes) [conditional]
  ├─→ migration-check (needs: changes) [conditional]
  └─→ test (needs: changes, lint, typecheck) [conditional]
       └─→ build (needs: changes, lint, typecheck, api-spec, test, migration-check) [conditional]
           ├─→ cargo-check-windows (needs: changes, lint) [conditional]
           │   └─→ build-desktop-windows (needs: changes, cargo-check-windows, typecheck) [conditional]
           ├─→ cargo-check-macos (needs: changes, lint) [conditional]
           │   └─→ build-desktop-macos (needs: changes, cargo-check-macos, typecheck) [conditional]
           └─→ cargo-check-linux (needs: changes, lint) [conditional]
               └─→ build-desktop-linux (needs: changes, cargo-check-linux, typecheck) [conditional]
```

### Changes Detection Job

**Name:** `changes`  
**Runs-on:** ubuntu-latest  
**Outputs:**
- `src` — True if source code changed
- `desktop` — True if desktop code changed

**Paths triggering `src`:**
- `apps/**`, `packages/**`, `tee-worker/**`, `tests/**`
- `.github/**`, `pnpm-lock.yaml`, `pnpm-workspace.yaml`, `tsconfig*.json`, `biome.json`

**Paths triggering `desktop`:**
- `apps/desktop/src/**`, `apps/desktop/src-tauri/src/**`, `apps/desktop/src-tauri/vendor/**`
- `apps/desktop/src-tauri/capabilities/**`, `apps/desktop/src-tauri/resources/**`
- `apps/desktop/src-tauri/Cargo.toml`, `apps/desktop/src-tauri/Cargo.lock`, `apps/desktop/src-tauri/build.rs`, `apps/desktop/src-tauri/rust-toolchain.toml`
- `apps/desktop/index.html`, `apps/desktop/vite.config.*`, `apps/desktop/tsconfig*`
- `packages/crypto/src/**`, `packages/crypto/tsconfig*`
- `.github/workflows/ci.yml`

### Core Jobs

#### `lint` (runs unconditionally)
- No dependencies
- Installs pnpm + Node 22, runs `pnpm lint`
- **Blocks:** typecheck, test, cargo-check-* (via `!failure()`)

#### `typecheck` (conditional: push OR src==true)
- **Condition:** `github.event_name == 'push' || needs.changes.outputs.src == 'true'`
- Runs `pnpm typecheck`

#### `api-spec` (conditional: push OR src==true)
- Services: PostgreSQL 16, IPFS Kubo v0.34.0
- Runs `pnpm api:generate` to rebuild OpenAPI spec and API client
- **Verifies:** Generated files in `apps/web/src/api/` and `packages/api-client/openapi.json` are up-to-date
- **Failure Mode:** If generated files aren't committed, CI fails

#### `migration-check` (conditional: push OR src==true)
- Service: PostgreSQL 16
- Runs TypeORM migration generator to detect entity/migration drift
- Filters out cosmetic constraint/index name differences
- **Failure Mode:** If structural changes detected without corresponding migrations

#### `test` (conditional: push OR src==true, depends on lint + typecheck)
- Services: PostgreSQL 16, IPFS Kubo v0.34.0
- Runs API tests (`pnpm --filter @cipherbox/api test:cov`)
- Runs Crypto tests (`pnpm --filter @cipherbox/crypto test:coverage`)
- **Uploads coverage to Codecov** (via `codecov/codecov-action@v5`)

#### `build` (conditional: push OR src==true, depends on all previous jobs)
- Builds crypto, API, and web packages only (excludes desktop)
- **Blocks:** If this fails, desktop builds don't start

### Desktop Build Pipeline

**Only runs if:** `github.event_name == 'push' OR needs.changes.outputs.desktop == 'true'`

#### `cargo-check-windows` (windows-latest)
- Installs WinFsp v2.1.25156 (hash-verified)
- Runs `cargo check --no-default-features --features winfsp`

#### `cargo-check-macos` (macos-latest)
- Installs FUSE-T via brew
- Runs `cargo check --no-default-features --features fuse`

#### `cargo-check-linux` (ubuntu-22.04)
- Installs libfuse3-dev and other system deps
- Runs `cargo check --no-default-features --features fuse`

#### `build-desktop-windows`
- Depends on: cargo-check-windows, typecheck
- Installs WinFsp (hash-verified)
- Runs `tauri-apps/tauri-action@v0` with `--features winfsp`
- Builds debug binary for E2E: `cargo build --no-default-features --features winfsp`
- **Uploads artifact:** `desktop-debug-windows` (retention: 1 day)

#### `build-desktop-macos`
- Depends on: cargo-check-macos, typecheck
- Installs FUSE-T via brew
- Runs `tauri-apps/tauri-action@v0` with `--features fuse`
- Builds debug binary for E2E: `cargo build --no-default-features --features fuse`
- **Uploads artifact:** `desktop-debug-macos` (retention: 1 day)

#### `build-desktop-linux`
- Depends on: cargo-check-linux, typecheck
- Installs system deps (libwebkit2gtk-4.1-dev, fuse3, etc.)
- Runs `tauri-apps/tauri-action@v0` with `--features fuse`
- Builds debug binary for E2E: `cargo build --no-default-features --features fuse`
- **Uploads artifact:** `desktop-debug-linux` (retention: 1 day)

---

## 2. E2E Web Tests (`e2e.yml`)

**Trigger:** `push` to `main`, `pull_request` to `main`, or manual `workflow_dispatch`

**Runs-on:** ubuntu-latest (15-minute timeout)

**Services:** PostgreSQL 16, IPFS v0.34.0, Redis 7

**Key Steps:**
1. Sets up Node 22 + pnpm 10
2. Installs Playwright browsers (Chromium)
3. Builds mock-ipns-routing tool
4. Builds crypto and API packages
5. Creates `.env` files for Web3Auth
6. Runs `pnpm --filter @cipherbox/e2e test`

**Environment Variables:**
- Database: PostgreSQL 16 (localhost:5432)
- IPFS: Kubo v0.34.0 (localhost:5001 API, 8080 gateway)
- Redis: localhost:6379
- API URL: http://localhost:3000
- IPNS routing: http://localhost:3001 (mock service)

**Artifacts on Failure:**
- `playwright-report/` and `test-results/` (7-day retention)

---

## 3. Desktop E2E Tests (`e2e-desktop.yml`)

**Trigger:** `workflow_run` (after CI completes successfully) or manual `workflow_dispatch`

**Runs on:** `macos-latest`, `windows-latest`, `ubuntu-22.04` (matrix, fail-fast: false, 30-minute timeout)

**Workflow Dispatch Option:** Can specify CI `run_id` to download binaries from; auto-finds latest if not specified

### Per-Platform Setup:

#### macOS
- Installs FUSE-T via brew
- Installs Redis via brew
- Backend: FUSE-T (SMB backend), uses `diskutil unmount force` for cleanup
- Artifact: `desktop-debug-macos`

#### Linux
- System deps: libfuse3-dev, fuse3, libwebkit2gtk-4.1-0, libayatana-appindicator3-1
- Installs Redis via apt
- Uses xvfb for display (via `GabrielBB/xvfb-action@v1`)
- Backend: Kernel FUSE, uses `fusermount3 -u` for cleanup
- Artifact: `desktop-debug-linux`

#### Windows
- Installs WinFsp via MSI (hash-verified)
- Installs Redis via Chocolatey (v6.4)
- Backend: SMB via WinFsp
- Uses PowerShell scripts for env setup
- Artifact: `desktop-debug-windows`

### Common Test Sequence:
1. Downloads debug binary from CI artifact
2. Sets up PostgreSQL (via ikalnytskyi/action-setup-postgres)
3. Installs Kubo IPFS daemon
4. Starts Redis
5. Installs Node 22 + pnpm 10
6. Builds crypto and API packages
7. Runs migrations
8. Starts mock IPNS routing service
9. Starts API server on port 3000
10. Launches desktop app with `--dev-key <random-32-byte-hex>`
11. Runs platform-specific test suite: `bash tests/e2e-desktop/scripts/run-all.sh` or `tests/e2e-desktop/scripts/run-all.ps1`
12. Cleans up desktop and FUSE/SMB mounts

**Failure Artifacts:**
- API logs, FUSE-T logs (7-day retention)

---

## 4. Staging Deployment (`deploy-staging.yml`)

**Trigger:**
- Push tag matching `v*-staging*` (git tag trigger)
- `workflow_call` from `tag-staging.yml`

**Environment:** `staging`

### Jobs (run in sequence):

#### `build-api` (ubuntu-latest)
- Builds Docker image from `apps/api/Dockerfile`
- Pushes to `ghcr.io/{OWNER}/cipherbox-api:{TAG}` and `:latest`

#### `build-tee` (ubuntu-latest)
- Builds Docker image from `tee-worker/Dockerfile`
- Pushes to `ghcr.io/{OWNER}/cipherbox-tee-worker:{TAG}` and `:latest`

#### `build-web` (ubuntu-latest, environment: staging)
- Uses staging environment vars: `VITE_API_URL`, `VITE_WEB3AUTH_CLIENT_ID`, etc.
- Builds crypto, then web package
- **Uploads artifact:** `web-dist` (1-day retention)

#### `build-desktop` macOS + Windows (environment: staging)
- macOS: Installs FUSE-T, builds with `--features fuse`
- Windows: Installs WinFsp, builds with `--features winfsp`
- Uses `tauri-apps/tauri-action@v0`
- Creates GitHub Release (prerelease) with built binaries

#### `deploy-vps` (depends on build-api, build-tee, build-web)
- Host: `vars.STAGING_HOST` (76.13.151.200)
- Generates `.env.staging` from secrets/vars
- SCP copies: `.env.staging`, `docker-compose.staging.yml`, `Caddyfile`, `alloy-config.river`
- SCP copies web dist to `/opt/cipherbox/web`
- SSH script:
  - Moves files to correct locations
  - Copies `.env.staging` → `.env` (for Docker Compose substitution)
  - Sets permissions to 600
  - Creates symlink for Caddy certs
  - Logs into GHCR
  - Runs `docker compose pull` (pulls new images)
  - Runs migrations: `docker compose run --rm api node dist/run-migrations.js`
  - Starts/restarts services: `docker compose up -d`
  - Restarts Caddy to pick up new web files
  - Prunes old images

---

## 5. Tag Staging Release (`tag-staging.yml`)

**Trigger:** Manual `workflow_dispatch` with input `release_tag` (e.g., `v0.3.0`)

**Jobs:**

#### `tag-staging` (ubuntu-latest, environment: staging-approval)
- Validates release tag exists via git
- Calculates next RC number by finding existing `{TAG}-staging-rc-*` tags
- **Output:** `staging_tag` (e.g., `v0.3.0-staging-rc-1`)
- Creates and pushes staging tag: `git tag {STAGING_TAG} {RELEASE_TAG}`

#### `deploy` (calls deploy-staging.yml)
- **Needs:** tag-staging outputs
- Uses `workflow_call` to invoke deploy-staging.yml with `staging_tag` parameter

**Convention:** Staging tags are named `v<version>-staging-rc-<N>` (e.g., `v0.1.2-staging-rc-1`)

---

## 6. Release Please (`release-please.yml`)

**Trigger:** `push` to `main`

**Single Job:** `release-please` (ubuntu-latest)

**Process:**
1. Uses GitHub App token (via `actions/create-github-app-token@v1`)
2. Runs `googleapis/release-please-action@v4`
3. Config files:
   - `release-please-config.json` (version bump rules, changelog generation)
   - `.release-please-manifest.json` (current version tracking)
4. Creates PR with version bumps and changelog
5. When PR merged, Release Please creates GitHub Release and `vX.Y.Z` tag

**Version Propagation:**
- All packages share unified version
- Bumps: `package.json` (all), `Cargo.toml`, `tauri.conf.json`

**Release Push:** Triggers any workflows listening for tag pushes (e.g., staging deploy if tag matches `v*-staging*`)

---

## 7. PR Title Validation (`pr-title.yml`)

**Trigger:** `pull_request` (opened, edited, synchronize)

**Validation Rules:**
- Must follow Conventional Commits: `<type>(<optional-scope>): <description>`
- Valid types: feat, fix, docs, style, refactor, perf, test, build, ci, chore, revert
- **CRITICAL:** Description must NOT contain parentheses (e.g., `(some words)`) — Release Please's parser misinterprets them

---

## 8. Claude Code Integration (`claude-code.yml`)

**Trigger:** Issue comments, PR review comments, PR reviews, or new issues containing `@claude`

**Permissions:** write access to contents, pull-requests, issues; read on actions

**Action:** `anthropics/claude-code-action@v1`

---

## Key Architectural Patterns

### 1. Changes Detection Pattern
- Single `changes` job detects which parts changed
- All downstream jobs use `if: github.event_name == 'push' || needs.changes.outputs.{src|desktop} == 'true'`
- Avoids running expensive jobs (typecheck, test, builds) on PRs with only doc changes

### 2. Desktop Build Parallelization
- Three independent cargo-check jobs (Windows, macOS, Linux) run in parallel
- Each cargo-check feeds into its own build job
- All use platform-specific FUSE driver + crypto+dependencies
- Tauri handles release binary creation; separate debug binary build for E2E

### 3. Staging Deployment
- Staging is triggered by git tags matching `v*-staging*`
- CI builds debug binaries (1-day retention) for E2E
- Staging release builds full release binaries and creates GitHub Release
- Docker images pushed to GHCR with tag + latest
- VPS deployment via SSH + SCP using Caddy reverse proxy

### 4. E2E Test Infrastructure
- Web E2E: Runs on Ubuntu with services (PostgreSQL, IPFS, Redis)
- Desktop E2E: Triggered after CI succeeds, runs on all three platforms
- Both use same mock-ipns-routing tool for delegated routing
- Desktop E2E downloads binaries from CI artifacts (1-day window)

### 5. Release Process
- Release Please automates version bumping + changelog
- Single unified version across all packages
- Manual staging tag creation via `tag-staging.yml` → auto-deploys via `deploy-staging.yml`
- Production release via Release Please PR → GitHub Release + tag

---

## Critical Sequences

### PR → Main → Production
1. PR triggers: ci.yml (lint, typecheck, test, build)
2. PR title validated (Conventional Commits format)
3. Merge to main triggers: release-please.yml
4. Release Please creates PR with version bumps
5. Merge release PR → GitHub Release + vX.Y.Z tag
6. Tag push → Deploy staging (if tag matches `v*-staging*`)

### Manual Staging Deploy
1. Run tag-staging.yml manually with release tag (e.g., v0.3.0)
2. tag-staging calculates next RC number
3. Creates and pushes `v0.3.0-staging-rc-1` tag
4. Tag push triggers deploy-staging.yml
5. Builds API, TEE, Web Docker images + Desktop binaries
6. Deploys via SSH/SCP to staging VPS

### Desktop E2E Testing
1. CI builds debug binaries for all three platforms (1-day retention)
2. e2e-desktop.yml triggered after CI completes
3. Downloads binary for each platform from CI artifacts
4. Sets up backend services (PostgreSQL, IPFS, Redis)
5. Launches desktop app with random dev key
6. Runs platform-specific test script

---

## File Paths Reference

- **Workflows:** `/Users/michael/Code/cipher-box/.github/workflows/*.yml`
- **Desktop code:** `apps/desktop/src/`, `apps/desktop/src-tauri/`
- **API:** `apps/api/`
- **Web:** `apps/web/`
- **Crypto package:** `packages/crypto/`
- **E2E web tests:** `tests/e2e/`
- **E2E desktop tests:** `tests/e2e-desktop/`
- **Mock IPNS routing:** `tools/mock-ipns-routing/`
- **TEE worker:** `tee-worker/`
- **Docker files:** `apps/api/Dockerfile`, `tee-worker/Dockerfile`, `docker/docker-compose.staging.yml`

This is the complete CI/CD pipeline structure for CipherBox.

  "content": "# Plan: Restructure Desktop CI Pipeline\n\n## Context\n\nThe desktop build jobs in `ci.yml` currently run on both PRs and pushes to main. Each platform builds **twice** per run — once via `tauri-action` (release) and once via `cargo build` (debug for E2E). This is slow (~10+ min per platform) and blocks PR merges unnecessarily. The macOS debug build is also currently broken due to a `PKG_CONFIG_PATH` issue.\n\n**Goal:** Fast PR CI with only compilation checks; full desktop builds only on main where they feed into E2E testing.\n\n## Current Flow\n\n```\nPR push → ci.yml:\n  lint, typecheck, test, build (web/api)\n  cargo-check (3 platforms)         ← fast (~2-3 min)\n  build-desktop (3 platforms)       ← slow (~10+ min each, runs tauri-action + debug build)\n\nMerge to main → ci.yml runs again → e2e-desktop.yml triggers (downloads debug artifacts, runs E2E)\n\nStaging tag → deploy-staging.yml (builds production release binaries via tauri-action)\n```\n\n## Proposed Flow\n\n```\nPR push → ci.yml:\n  lint, typecheck, test, build (web/api)\n  cargo-check (3 platforms)         ← validates compilation, fast\n\nMerge to main → ci.yml:\n  same as PR + build-desktop (3 platforms, debug only)\n  → e2e-desktop.yml triggers (downloads debug artifacts, runs E2E)\n\nStaging tag → deploy-staging.yml (builds production release binaries, unchanged)\n```\n\n## Changes\n\n### 1. Remove `tauri-action` from desktop build jobs in `ci.yml`\n\nThe `tauri-action` step does a full release build in each desktop job. This is redundant — `deploy-staging.yml` already has its own `tauri-action` for production releases. Remove it from all 3 platform build jobs, keeping only the debug binary build + artifact upload.\n\n**Files:** `.github/workflows/ci.yml` (lines ~566, ~631, ~510 area)\n\n### 2. Gate desktop build jobs to only run on push to main\n\nChange the `if` condition on `build-desktop-{macos,windows,linux}` from:\n```yaml\nif: |\n  !failure() && !cancelled() &&\n  (github.event_name == 'push' || needs.changes.outputs.desktop == 'true')\n```\nto:\n```yaml\nif: |\n  !failure() && !cancelled() &&\n  github.event_name == 'push' &&\n  needs.changes.outputs.desktop == 'true'\n```\n\nThis means desktop builds only happen on pushes to main with desktop changes. PRs still get `cargo-check` validation.\n\n### 3. Fix macOS debug build `PKG_CONFIG_PATH`\n\nThe existing fix from the previous commit needs to stay (already done), but also remove the `tauri-action` step which was masking the issue via cached artifacts.\n\n### 4. Remove `Build {macOS,Windows,Linux} Desktop` from required PR status checks\n\nSince these jobs won't run on PRs anymore, remove them from branch protection. The `Cargo Check` jobs remain as required checks.\n\n**Method:** `gh api` call to update branch protection rules.\n\n### 5. Remove Cargo cache for desktop builds (optional cleanup)\n\nWith tauri-action removed, the cargo cache in desktop build jobs only needs to cache the debug build. The cache key and paths remain the same but the cache will be smaller without release artifacts.\n\n## Files Modified\n\n1. `.github/workflows/ci.yml` — Remove tauri-action steps, change if-conditions on build-desktop jobs\n2. Branch protection rules — Remove 3 build checks via GitHub API\n\n## Verification\n\n1. Push the changes on this branch — verify PR CI skips desktop build jobs (only cargo-check runs)\n2. Check that cargo-check jobs still run and pass on PR\n3. After merge, verify desktop build jobs run on main push and upload debug artifacts\n4. ## Approved Plan:
# Plan: Restructure Desktop CI Pipeline

## Context

The desktop build jobs in `ci.yml` currently run on both PRs and pushes to main. Each platform builds **twice** per run — once via `tauri-action` (release) and once via `cargo build` (debug for E2E). This is slow (~10+ min per platform) and blocks PR merges unnecessarily. The macOS debug build is also currently broken due to a `PKG_CONFIG_PATH` issue.

**Goal:** Fast PR CI with only compilation checks; full desktop builds only on main where they feed into E2E testing.

## Current Flow

```
PR push → ci.yml:
  lint, typecheck, test, build (web/api)
  cargo-check (3 platforms)         ← fast (~2-3 min)
  build-desktop (3 platforms)       ← slow (~10+ min each, runs tauri-action + debug build)

Merge to main → ci.yml runs again → e2e-desktop.yml triggers (downloads debug artifacts, runs E2E)

Staging tag → deploy-staging.yml (builds production release binaries via tauri-action)
```

## Proposed Flow

```
PR push → ci.yml:
  lint, typecheck, test, build (web/api)
  cargo-check (3 platforms)         ← validates compilation, fast

Merge to main → ci.yml:
  same as PR + build-desktop (3 platforms, debug only)
  → e2e-desktop.yml triggers (downloads debug artifacts, runs E2E)

Staging tag → deploy-staging.yml (builds production release binaries, unchanged)
```

## Changes

### 1. Remove `tauri-action` from desktop build jobs in `ci.yml`

The `tauri-action` step does a full release build in each desktop job. This is redundant — `deploy-staging.yml` already has its own `tauri-action` for production releases. Remove it from all 3 platform build jobs, keeping only the debug binary build + artifact upload.

**Files:** `.github/workflows/ci.yml` (lines ~566, ~631, ~510 area)

### 2. Gate desktop build jobs to only run on push to main

Change the `if` condition on `build-desktop-{macos,windows,linux}` from:
```yaml
if: |
  !failure() && !cancelled() &&
  (github.event_name == 'push' || needs.changes.outputs.desktop == 'true')
```
to:
```yaml
if: |
  !failure() && !cancelled() &&
  github.event_name == 'push' &&
  needs.changes.outputs.desktop == 'true'
```

This means desktop builds only happen on pushes to main with desktop changes. PRs still get `cargo-check` validation.

### 3. Fix macOS debug build `PKG_CONFIG_PATH`

The existing fix from the previous commit needs to stay (already done), but also remove the `tauri-action` step which was masking the issue via cached artifacts.

### 4. Remove `Build {macOS,Windows,Linux} Desktop` from required PR status checks

Since these jobs won't run on PRs anymore, remove them from branch protection. The `Cargo Check` jobs remain as required checks.

**Method:** `gh api` call to update branch protection rules.

### 5. Remove Cargo cache for desktop builds (optional cleanup)

With tauri-action removed, the cargo cache in desktop build jobs only needs to cache the debug build. The cache key and paths remain the same but the cache will be smaller without release artifacts.

## Files Modified

1. `.github/workflows/ci.yml` — Remove tauri-action steps, change if-conditions on build-desktop jobs
2. Branch protection rules — Remove 3 build checks via GitHub API

## Verification

1. Push the changes on this branch — verify PR CI skips desktop build jobs (only cargo-check runs)
2. Check that cargo-check jobs still run and pass on PR
3. After merge, verify desktop build jobs run on main push and upload debug artifacts
4. Verify e2e-desktop.yml can still download and use the artifacts

```

> AGENT

All done. Here's a summary of what was completed:

**Commit `d7356a6f6`** — `ci: remove tauri-action from desktop builds and gate to main only`

**Changes to `.github/workflows/ci.yml`:**
- Removed `tauri-action` and its prerequisites (pnpm, node, install, crypto build) from all 3 desktop build jobs — saves ~10 min per platform
- Gated desktop builds to only run on `push` to main (not on PRs) — PRs still get fast `cargo-check` validation

**Branch protection updated:**
- Removed: `Build Windows Desktop`, `Build macOS Desktop`, `Build Linux Desktop`
- Kept: `Cargo Check (Windows)`, `Cargo Check (macOS)`, plus all other existing checks

**New CI flow:**
- **PR** → lint, typecheck, test, build (web/api), cargo-check (3 platforms) — fast
- **Push to main** → same + debug desktop builds → triggers e2e-desktop.yml
- **Staging tag** → deploy-staging.yml builds production releases (unchanged)

     1→# test-round-trip.ps1 -- Verify desktop FUSE writes are visible via the API (Windows).
     2→#
     3→# Usage: .\test-round-trip.ps1 [-MountPoint <path>] [-ApiUrl <url>] [-TestSecret <secret>]
     4→#   -MountPoint   Path to FUSE mount (default: $env:USERPROFILE\CipherBox)
     5→#   -ApiUrl       Backend API URL (default: http://localhost:3000)
     6→#   -TestSecret   Shared secret for test-login (default: e2e-test-secret-ci-only)
     7→#
     8→# The server is zero-knowledge -- it cannot decrypt file contents.
     9→# These tests prove the pipeline: FUSE write -> encrypt -> IPFS upload -> IPNS publish -> API visibility.
    10→#
    11→# Exit code: number of failed tests (0 = all passed).
    12→
    13→param(

    17→

    20→$ErrorActionPreference = "Continue"
    21→
    22→$TestEmail = "e2e-desktop-rt-$([DateTimeOffset]::UtcNow.ToUnixTimeSeconds())@test.local"
    23→
    24→$Pass = 0
    25→$Fail = 0
    26→
    27→function Test-Pass {
    28→    param([string]$Name)
    29→    $script:Pass++
    30→    Write-Host "PASS: $Name"
    31→}
    32→
    33→function Test-Fail {
    34→    param([string]$Name)
    35→    $script:Fail++
    36→    Write-Host "FAIL: $Name"
    37→}
    38→
    39→Write-Host "=== API Round-Trip Tests ==="
    40→Write-Host "Mount point: $MountPoint"
    41→Write-Host "API URL:     $ApiUrl"
    42→Write-Host "Test email:  $TestEmail"
    43→Write-Host ""
    44→
    45→# ---- Test 1: Authenticate via test-login ----
    46→Write-Host "--- Test 1: Authenticate via test-login ---"
    47→$Body = @{
    48→    email  = $TestEmail

    51→
    52→try {
    53→    $AuthResponse = Invoke-RestMethod -Uri "$ApiUrl/auth/test-login" `

    58→    $AccessToken = $AuthResponse.accessToken

    64→        Write-Host ""
    65→        Write-Host "=== API Round-Trip Results ==="
    66→        Write-Host "  Passed: $Pass"

    69→        exit $Fail
    70→    }
    71→} catch {

    75→    Write-Host "=== API Round-Trip Results ==="
    76→    Write-Host "  Passed: $Pass"

    80→}
    81→

    83→
    84→# ---- Test 2: Desktop writes file, API verifies vault exists ----

    99→            -Headers $Headers
   100→

   102→        if ($RootIpns) {
   103→            Test-Pass "Vault has rootIpnsName after FUSE write ($RootIpns)"
   104→        } else {

   106→        }
   107→    } catch {
   108→        Test-Fail "Vault API call failed ($_)"
   109→        $RootIpns = $null
   110→    }

   115→

   133→    }

   136→}
   137→
   138→# ---- Cleanup ----

   143→# ---- Summary ----

   146→Write-Host "  Passed: $Pass"
   147→Write-Host "  Failed: $Fail"
   148→Write-Host "==============================="
   149→
   150→exit $Fail
   151→

     1→#!/usr/bin/env bash
     2→set -euo pipefail
     3→
     4→# test-round-trip.sh -- Verify desktop FUSE writes are visible via the API.
     5→#
     6→# Usage: ./test-round-trip.sh [mount-point] [api-url] [test-secret]
     7→#   mount-point  Path to FUSE mount (default: $HOME/CipherBox)
     8→#   api-url      Backend API URL (default: http://localhost:3000)
     9→#   test-secret  Shared secret for test-login (default: e2e-test-secret-ci-only)
    10→#
    11→# The server is zero-knowledge -- it cannot decrypt file contents.
    12→# These tests prove the pipeline: FUSE write -> encrypt -> IPFS upload -> IPNS publish -> API visibility.
    13→#
    14→# Exit code: number of failed tests (0 = all passed).
    15→
    16→MP="${1:-$HOME/CipherBox}"
    17→API_URL="${2:-http://localhost:3000}"
    18→SECRET="${TEST_SECRET=[REDACTED]}"
    19→TEST_EMAIL="e2e-desktop-rt-$(date +%s)@test.local"
    20→
    21→PASS=0
    22→FAIL=0
    23→
    24→pass() {
    25→  PASS=$((PASS + 1))
    26→  echo "PASS: $1"
    27→}
    28→
    29→fail() {
    30→  FAIL=$((FAIL + 1))
    31→  echo "FAIL: $1"
    32→}
    33→
    34→echo "=== API Round-Trip Tests ==="
    35→echo "Mount point: $MP"
    36→echo "API URL:     $API_URL"
    37→echo "Test email:  $TEST_EMAIL"
    38→echo ""
    39→
    40→# ---- Test 1: Authenticate via test-login ----
    41→echo "--- Test 1: Authenticate via test-login ---"
    42→AUTH_RESPONSE=$(curl -fsS --connect-timeout 5 --max-time 30 -X POST "$API_URL/auth/test-login" \
    43→  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
    44→  -d "{\"email\":\"$TEST_EMAIL\",\"secret\":\"$SECRET\"}" 2>&1) || true
    45→
    46→ACCESS_TOKEN=$(echo "$AUTH_RESPONSE" | jq -r '.accessToken // empty')
    47→if [ -n "$ACCESS_TOKEN" ]; then
    48→  pass "Authenticate via test-login"
    49→else
    50→  AUTH_ERROR=$(echo "$AUTH_RESPONSE" | jq -r '.message // .error // empty' 2>/dev/null || echo "non-JSON response")
    51→  fail "Authenticate via test-login (error: $AUTH_ERROR)"
    52→  echo "FATAL: Cannot proceed without authentication."
    53→  echo ""
    54→  echo "=== API Round-Trip Results ==="
    55→  echo "  Passed: $PASS"
    56→  echo "  Failed: $FAIL"
    57→  echo "==============================="
    58→  exit "$FAIL"
    59→fi
    60→
    61→# ---- Test 2: Desktop writes file, API verifies vault exists ----
    62→echo "--- Test 2: Verify vault has content after FUSE write ---"
    63→echo "API-visible content" > "$MP/rt-test.txt"
    64→sleep 5
    65→
    66→VAULT_RESPONSE=$(curl -fsS --connect-timeout 5 --max-time 30 -H "Authorization: Bearer $ACCESS_TOKEN" \
    67→  "$API_URL/vault" 2>&1) || true
    68→
    69→ROOT_IPNS=$(echo "$VAULT_RESPONSE" | jq -r '.rootIpnsName // empty')
    70→if [ -n "$ROOT_IPNS" ] && [ "$ROOT_IPNS" != "null" ]; then
    71→  pass "Vault has rootIpnsName after FUSE write ($ROOT_IPNS)"
    72→else
    73→  VAULT_ERROR=$(echo "$VAULT_RESPONSE" | jq -r '.message // .error // empty' 2>/dev/null || echo "non-JSON response")
    74→  fail "Vault has no rootIpnsName (error: $VAULT_ERROR)"
    75→fi
    76→
    77→# ---- Test 3: Verify IPNS resolve returns data ----
    78→echo "--- Test 3: Verify IPNS resolve returns CID ---"
    79→if [ -n "$ROOT_IPNS" ] && [ "$ROOT_IPNS" != "null" ]; then
    80→  IPNS_RESPONSE=$(curl -fsS --connect-timeout 5 --max-time 30 -H "Authorization: Bearer $ACCESS_TOKEN" \
    81→    "$API_URL/ipns/$ROOT_IPNS/resolve" 2>&1) || true
    82→
    83→  # The resolve response should contain a CID (starts with "bafy" or "Qm" or similar)
    84→  RESOLVED_CID=$(echo "$IPNS_RESPONSE" | jq -r '.cid // .value // empty' 2>/dev/null || echo "")
    85→  if [ -n "$RESOLVED_CID" ] && [ "$RESOLVED_CID" != "null" ]; then
    86→    pass "IPNS resolve returned CID ($RESOLVED_CID)"
    87→  else
    88→    # Even if parsing fails, the response itself may be valid
    89→    IPNS_ERROR=$(echo "$IPNS_RESPONSE" | jq -r '.message // .error // empty' 2>/dev/null || echo "non-JSON response")
    90→    fail "IPNS resolve did not return expected CID (error: $IPNS_ERROR)"
    91→  fi
    92→else
    93→  fail "IPNS resolve skipped (no rootIpnsName)"
    94→fi
    95→
    96→# ---- Cleanup ----
    97→echo "--- Cleanup ---"
    98→rm -f "$MP/rt-test.txt" 2>/dev/null || true
    99→sleep 2
   100→
   101→# ---- Summary ----
   102→echo ""
   103→echo "=== API Round-Trip Results ==="
   104→echo "  Passed: $PASS"
   105→echo "  Failed: $FAIL"
   106→echo "==============================="
   107→
   108→exit "$FAIL"
   109→

([github.blog](https://github.blog/changelog/2025-09-19-github-actions-macos-13-runner-image-is-closing-down?utm_source=openai))  \n- **Feb 26, 2026:** **`macos-26` became GA**, with explicit labels for **arm64 vs Intel**. ([github.blog](https://github.blog/changelog/2026-02-26-macos-26-is-now-generally-available-for-github-hosted-runners/?utm_source=openai))  \n\n### How to select architecture explicitly\n- **Apple Silicon (arm64):**\n  - `macos-latest` (currently `macos-15-arm64`) ([github.com](https://github.com/actions/runner-images?utm_source=openai))\n  - `macos-15`, `macos-26` ([docs.github.com](https://docs.github.com/actions/reference/runners/github-hosted-runners?utm_source=openai))\n- **Intel (x64):**\n  - `macos-15-intel` ([docs.github.com](https://docs.github.com/actions/reference/runners/github-hosted-runners?utm_source=openai))\n  - `macos-26-intel` ([github.blog](https://github.blog/changelog/2026-02-26-macos-26-is-now-generally-available-for-github-hosted-runners/?utm_source=openai))\n\n### Runner “size” labels (common source of confusion)\n- On newer images, **xlarge is arm64** and **large is typically Intel** (e.g., `macos-26-xlarge` is arm64; `macos-26-large` is x64). ([github.blog](https://github.blog/changelog/2026-02-26-macos-26-is-now-generally-available-for-github-hosted-runners/?utm_source=openai))\n\nCitations:\n\n- 1: https://docs.github.com/actions/reference/runners/github-hosted-runners?utm_source=openai\n- 2: https://github.com/actions/runner-images/issues/12520?utm_source=openai\n- 3: https://github.blog/changelog/2025-09-19-github-actions-macos-13-runner-image-is-closing-down?utm_source=openai\n- 4: https://github.blog/changelog/2026-02-26-macos-26-is-now-generally-available-for-github-hosted-runners/?utm_source=openai\n- 5: https://github.com/actions/runner-images?utm_source=openai\n- 6: https://docs.github.com/actions/reference/runners/github-hosted-runners?utm_source=openai\n- 7: https://docs.github.com/actions/reference/runners/github-hosted-runners?utm_source=openai\n- 8: https://github.blog/changelog/2026-02-26-macos-26-is-now-generally-available-for-github-hosted-runners/?utm_source=openai\n- 9: https://github.blog/changelog/2026-02-26-macos-26-is-now-generally-available-for-github-hosted-runners/?utm_source=openai\n\n---\n\n🏁 Script executed:\n\n```shell\ncd .github/workflows && wc -l ci.yml && sed -n '530,540p' ci.yml\n```\n\nRepository: FSM1/cipher-box\n\nLength of output: 460\n\n---\n\n🏁 Script executed:\n\n```shell\n# Check which runner/job this step is part of\nsed -n '500,550p' .github/workflows/ci.yml | head -60\n```\n\nRepository: FSM1/cipher-box\n\nLength of output: 1649\n\n---\n\n\n\n</details>\n\n**PKG_CONFIG_PATH must be updated for Apple Silicon Homebrew paths.**\n\nThe `macos-latest` runner uses Apple Silicon (M1/M2), where Homebrew installs to `/opt/homebrew/` rather than `/usr/local/`. The current `PKG_CONFIG_PATH: /usr/local/lib/pkgconfig` will fail to locate FUSE-T headers and libraries, causing the cargo build to fail.\n\n<details>\n<summary>Suggested fix</summary>\n\n```diff\n       - name: Build debug binary for E2E\n         run: cargo build --manifest-path apps/desktop/src-tauri/Cargo.toml --no-default-features --features fuse\n         env:\n-          PKG_CONFIG_PATH: /usr/local/lib/pkgconfig\n+          PKG_CONFIG_PATH: /opt/homebrew/lib/pkgconfig:/usr/local/lib/pkgconfig\n```\n</details>\n\n<details>\n<summary>🤖 Prompt for AI Agents</summary>\n\n```\nVerify each finding against the current code and only fix it if needed.\n\nIn @.github/workflows/ci.yml around lines 532 - 536, The CI step \"Build debug\nbinary for E2E\" sets PKG_CONFIG_PATH to /usr/local/lib/pkgconfig which fails on\nApple Silicon; update that environment setting so PKG_CONFIG_PATH includes the\nApple Silicon Homebrew path (/opt/homebrew/lib/pkgconfig) in addition to\n/usr/local/lib/pkgconfig (or set it to a combination like\n\"/opt/homebrew/lib/pkgconfig:/usr/local/lib/pkgconfig\") so cargo can find FUSE-T\nheaders/libraries on macos-latest runners.\n```\n\n</details>\n\n<!-- This is an auto-generated comment by CodeRabbit -->",

> AGENT

The comment is valid — `macos-latest` is Apple Silicon where Homebrew installs to `/opt/homebrew/`. While FUSE-T specifically installs its `fuse.pc` to `/usr/local/lib/pkgconfig/`, including both paths is a safe defensive measure for any Homebrew-based pkg-config dependencies.

> AGENT

Here's my take:

**I'd support gating it to push-only**, for the same reasons as desktop. The web E2E suite spins up 3 service containers (Postgres, IPFS/Kubo, Redis), builds the full stack, installs Playwright browsers, and runs the tests — that's easily 5-10 minutes. On PRs you're already running lint, typecheck, unit tests, API spec verification, and cargo checks. Those catch the vast majority of issues.

The tradeoff is minor: if a PR breaks an E2E flow, you'd only find out after merge to main rather than during review. But in practice:
- The unit test + typecheck + API spec combo catches most regressions
- E2E failures on main are caught before desktop E2E and staging deploy run
- PRs merge faster, which matters more day-to-day

The change would be simple — just tighten the `if` on the `e2e` job from:

```yaml
if: |
  github.event_name == 'push' || github.event_name == 'workflow_dispatch' ||
  needs.changes.outputs.src == 'true'
```

to:

```yaml
if: |
  github.event_name == 'push' || github.event_name == 'workflow_dispatch'
```

And remove `E2E Tests` from the required status checks on main. Want me to go ahead?
